--- CHUNK 1 to 587 ---
1: import { useContext, useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
2: import { AppContext } from '../context/AppContext';
3: import { LineChart, Line, XAxis, YAxis, CartesianGrid, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer } from 'recharts';
4: 
5: function Analysis() {
6:   const {
7:     API_BASE_URL, prepSteps, status, setStatus,
8:     movieTitle, setMovieTitle, numTopics, setNumTopics,
9:     analysisResult, setAnalysisResult,
10:     optimalKResults, setOptimalKResults,
11:     isSearchingK, setIsSearchingK,
12:     uploadedFilename,
13:     showToast,
14:   } = useContext(AppContext);
15: 
16:   const [suggestedK, setSuggestedK] = useState(null);
17:   const [bestPerplexityK, setBestPerplexityK] = useState(null);
18:   const [interpretations, setInterpretations] = useState({});
19:   const [isAnalyzing, setIsAnalyzing] = useState(false);
20:   const [kProgress, setKProgress] = useState({ current: 0, total: 9, currentK: 2 });
21: 
22:   const pollRef = useRef(null);
23: 
24:   useEffect(() => {
25:     return () => { if (pollRef.current) clearInterval(pollRef.current); };
26:   }, []);
27: 
28:   useEffect(() => {
29:     if (analysisResult?.topics) {
30:       const saved = analysisResult.interpretations || {};
31:       const initial = {};
32:       Object.entries(analysisResult.topics).forEach(([topicName, topicData]) => {
33:         initial[topicName] = saved[topicName] || {
34:           custom_label: topicData.auto_label || '',
35:           notes: topicData.auto_notes || ''
36:         };
37:       });
38:       setInterpretations(initial);
39:     } else {
40:       setInterpretations({});
41:     }
42:   }, [analysisResult]);
43: 
44:   const openFullScreen = () => {
45:     if (!analysisResult?.vis_html) return;
46:     const newWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
47:     if (newWindow) {
48:       newWindow.document.write(analysisResult.vis_html);
49:       const distData = analysisResult.overall_distribution;
50:       const scriptCode = `(function() {
51:         var dist = ${JSON.stringify(distData)};
52:         setInterval(function() {
53:           Object.keys(dist).forEach(function(key) {
54:             var num    = key.replace('Topik ', '');
55:             var circle = document.getElementById('topic' + num);
56:             if (circle) {
57:               var cx = circle.getAttribute('cx');
58:               var cy = circle.getAttribute('cy');
59:               var parent = circle.parentNode;
60:               if (parent) {
61:                 var texts = parent.querySelectorAll('text');
62:                 texts.forEach(function(t) {
63:                   if (t.textContent.trim() === String(num) && t.id.indexOf('custom-pct') === -1) {
64:                     t.style.display = 'none';
65:                   }
66:                 });
67:               }
68:               var customId   = 'custom-pct-' + num;
69:               var customText = document.getElementById(customId);
70:               if (!customText) {
71:                 customText = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "text");
72:                 customText.id = customId;
73:                 customText.setAttribute('text-anchor', 'middle');
74:                 customText.setAttribute('dominant-baseline', 'central');
75:                 customText.setAttribute('fill', '#242424');
76:                 customText.setAttribute('font-size', '16px');
77:                 customText.setAttribute('font-weight', 'bold');
78:                 customText.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
79:                 customText.textContent = dist[key] + '%';
80:                 parent.appendChild(customText);
81:               }
82:               if (cx && cy) {
83:                 customText.setAttribute('x', cx);
84:                 customText.setAttribute('y', cy);
85:               }
86:             }
87:           });
88:         }, 100);
89:       })();`;
90:       newWindow.document.write('<script>' + scriptCode + '<\/script>');
91:       newWindow.document.close();
92:     } else {
93:       showToast("Pop-up diblokir. Mohon izinkan pop-up pada browser Anda.", 'warning');
94:     }
95:   };
96: 
97:   //Jalankan LDA setelah K diketahui 
98:   const runAnalyze = async (bestK, bestMode, kResults) => {
99:     setIsAnalyzing(true);
100:     setStatus(`Melatih model LDA final dengan K=${bestK} (${bestMode})...`);
101:     setAnalysisResult(null);
102:     try {
103:       const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/analyze`, {
104:         method: 'POST',
105:         headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
106:         body: JSON.stringify({
107:           title: movieTitle.trim(),
108:           num_topics: bestK,
109:           mode: bestMode,
110:           filename: uploadedFilename,
111:           optimal_k_results: kResults
112:         })
113:       });
114:       if (!response.ok) {
115:         let errStr = `Error ${response.status}`;
116:         try { const e = await response.json(); errStr = e.error || errStr; } catch (_) { }
117:         setStatus(`Gagal Analisis: ${errStr}`);
118:         showToast(errStr, 'error');
119:         return;
120:       }
121:       const data = await response.json();
122:       if (data.status === 'success') {
123:         setAnalysisResult(data.data);
124:         setNumTopics(bestK);
125:         setStatus(`Selesai! Model LDA K=${bestK} berhasil dibangun.`);
126:         showToast(`Analisis LDA selesai dengan K=${bestK}!`, 'success');
127:       } else {
128:         setStatus(`Gagal Analisis: ${data.error}`);
129:         showToast(data.error, 'error');
130:       }
131:     } catch {
132:       setStatus("Gagal menghubungi server backend.");
133:       showToast("Tidak dapat terhubung ke server.", 'error');
134:     } finally {
135:       setIsAnalyzing(false);
136:     }
137:   };
138: 
139:   //Cari K → Analisis otomatis
140:   const handleFullAnalysis = async () => {
141:     if (!prepSteps) {
142:       showToast("Lakukan preprocessing terlebih dahulu.", 'warning');
143:       return;
144:     }
145:     if (!movieTitle || !movieTitle.trim()) {
146:       showToast("Isi Nama / Judul Dataset terlebih dahulu.", 'warning');
147:       return;
148:     }
149: 
150:     // Reset state
151:     setIsSearchingK(true);
152:     setOptimalKResults(null);
153:     setSuggestedK(null);
154:     setBestPerplexityK(null);
155:     setAnalysisResult(null);
156:     setKProgress({ current: 0, total: 9, currentK: 2 });
157:     setStatus("Memulai evaluasi Topik 2 sampai 10");
158: 
159:     try {
160:       // Langkah 1: Mulai background task cari K optimal
161:       const startRes = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/find_optimal_k`, {
162:         method: 'POST',
163:         headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
164:         body: JSON.stringify({
165:           min_k: 2, max_k: 10,
166:           filename: uploadedFilename,
167:           title: movieTitle.trim()
168:         })
169:       });
170: 
171:       const startData = await startRes.json();
172:       if (startData.status !== 'started') {
173:         showToast(startData.error || 'Gagal memulai kalkulasi.', 'error');
174:         setIsSearchingK(false);
175:         return;
176:       }
177: 
178:       const taskId = startData.task_id;
179: 
180:       // Langkah 2: Polling hingga selesai
181:       pollRef.current = setInterval(async () => {
182:         try {
183:           const statusRes = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/task_status/${taskId}`);
184:           const statusData = await statusRes.json();
185: 
186:           if (!statusData.data) {
187:             clearInterval(pollRef.current);
188:             setIsSearchingK(false);
189:             showToast('Gagal membaca status task.', 'error');
190:             return;
191:           }
192: 
193:           const task = statusData.data;
194: 
195:           if (task.status === 'running') {
196:             const done = task.progress + 1;
197:             setKProgress({ current: done, total: task.total, currentK: task.current_k });
198:             setStatus(`Mengevaluasi ${task.current_mode} K=${task.current_k} (${done}/${task.total})...`);
199: 
200:           } else if (task.status === 'done') {
201:             clearInterval(pollRef.current);
202:             setIsSearchingK(false);
203: 
204:             const results = task.result;
205:             const bestCoh = results.reduce((max, c) => c.score > max.score ? c : max, results[0]);
206:             const bestPerp = results.reduce((min, c) => c.perplexity < min.perplexity ? c : min, results[0]);
207: 
208:             setOptimalKResults(results);
209:             setSuggestedK(bestCoh);
210:             setBestPerplexityK(bestPerp);
211:             setStatus(`Evaluasi selesai. K optimal (Coherence): ${bestCoh.k} (${bestCoh.mode}). Melatih model final...`);
212:             showToast(`Evaluasi K selesai! Melanjutkan analisis dengan K=${bestCoh.k} (${bestCoh.mode})...`, 'info');
213: 
214:             // Langkah 3: Langsung jalankan analisis dengan K terbaik
215:             await runAnalyze(bestCoh.k, bestCoh.mode, results);
216: 
217:           } else if (task.status === 'error') {
218:             clearInterval(pollRef.current);
219:             setIsSearchingK(false);
220:             setStatus(`Gagal: ${task.error}`);
221:             showToast(`Gagal kalkulasi: ${task.error}`, 'error');
222:           }
223:         } catch {
224:           clearInterval(pollRef.current);
225:           setIsSearchingK(false);
226:           showToast('Gagal menghubungi server saat polling.', 'error');
227:         }
228:       }, 2000);
229: 
230:     } catch {
231:       setIsSearchingK(false);
232:       showToast('Gagal menghubungi server.', 'error');
233:     }
234:   };
235: 
236:   const isBusy = isSearchingK || isAnalyzing;
237:   const progressPct = kProgress.total > 0
238:     ? Math.round((kProgress.current / kProgress.total) * 100)
239:     : 0;
240: 
241:   // Label tombol sesuai fase
242:   const btnLabel = isSearchingK
243:     ? `Mengevaluasi K=${kProgress.currentK} (${kProgress.current}/${kProgress.total})…`
244:     : isAnalyzing
245:       ? 'Melatih Model LDA Final…'
246:       : 'Jalankan Analisis';
247: 
248:   return (
249:     <div>
250:       <h1 className="page-title">Analisis Topik (LDA)</h1>
251: 
252:       {(!prepSteps && !analysisResult) ? (
253:         <div className="card-container" style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '60px 20px' }}>
254:           <div style={{ fontSize: '40px', marginBottom: '15px' }}>⚙️</div>
255:           <h3 style={{ color: '#242424', margin: '0 0 10px 0', fontSize: '18px' }}>Data Belum Siap</h3>
256:           <p className="text-muted" style={{ marginBottom: '25px' }}>
257:             Anda harus menyelesaikan tahapan Preprocessing terlebih dahulu sebelum dapat membangun model LDA.
258:           </p>
259:         </div>
260:       ) : (
261:         <>
262:           {/* ── Panel Konfigurasi & Progress ── */}
263:           <div className="card-container">
264:             <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
265: 
266:               {/* Konfigurasi */}
267:               <div style={{ width: '100%' }}>
268:                 <h3 className="card-title">Konfigurasi Model</h3>
269:                 <div style={{ marginBottom: '15px' }}>
270:                   <input
271:                     type="text"
272:                     value={movieTitle}
273:                     onChange={(e) => setMovieTitle(e.target.value)}
274:                     placeholder="Judul Film"
275:                     style={{
276:                       width: '100%', padding: '10px', borderRadius: '6px',
277:                       border: '1px solid #e5e7eb', boxSizing: 'border-box'
278:                     }}
279:                   />
280:                 </div>
281: 
282:                 <button
283:                   onClick={handleFullAnalysis}
284:                   className="btn-primary"
285:                   style={{ width: '100%', padding: '12px', fontSize: '15px' }}
286:                   disabled={isBusy}
287:                 >
288:                   {btnLabel}
289:                 </button>
290: 
291:                 <p className="text-muted" style={{ marginTop: '12px', textAlign: 'center', fontSize: '13px' }}>
292:                   {status}
293:                 </p>
294:               </div>
295: 
296:               {/* Progress & Hasil Evaluasi K */}
297:               <div style={{
298:                 width: '100%', backgroundColor: '#f9fafb', padding: '20px',
299:                 borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #e5e7eb'
300:               }}>
301:                 <h3 className="card-title">Evaluasi Model (Coherence &amp; Perplexity)</h3>
302: 
303:                 {/* Progress bar saat evaluasi berjalan */}
304:                 {isSearchingK && (
305:                   <div className="progress-wrapper">
306:                     <p className="progress-label">
307:                       Mengevaluasi K={kProgress.currentK}&nbsp;
308:                       <span style={{ color: '#6b7280', fontWeight: 'normal' }}>
309:                         ({kProgress.current}/{kProgress.total} selesai)
310:                       </span>
311:                     </p>
312:                     <div className="progress-bar-track">
313:                       <div className="progress-bar-fill" style={{ width: `${progressPct}%` }} />
314:                     </div>
315:                     <p style={{ margin: '6px 0 0 0', fontSize: '12px', color: '#9ca3af', textAlign: 'right' }}>
316:                       {progressPct}%
317:                     </p>
318:                   </div>
319:                 )}
320: 
321:                 {/* Indikator fase analisis */}
322:                 {isAnalyzing && (
323:                   <div style={{
324:                     padding: '12px 16px', backgroundColor: '#eff6ff',
325:                     borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #bfdbfe', marginBottom: '16px'
326:                   }}>
327:                     <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '13px', color: '#1d4ed8', fontWeight: '600' }}>
328:                       Melatih model LDA final dengan K={numTopics}...
329:                     </p>
330:                   </div>
331:                 )}
332: 
333:                 {/* Tabel & Grafik hasil evaluasi */}
334:                 {optimalKResults && (
335:                   <div style={{ animation: 'fadeIn 0.5s ease-in-out' }}>
336:                     <h4 style={{ margin: '0 0 10px 0', fontSize: '14px', color: '#4b5563' }}>Tabel Hasil Kalkulasi</h4>
337:                     <div style={{
338:                       maxHeight: '180px', overflowY: 'auto', border: '1px solid #e5e7eb',
339:                       borderRadius: '6px', marginBottom: '20px'
340:                     }}>
341:                       <table style={{
342:                         width: '100%', fontSize: '12px', backgroundColor: '#fff',
343:                         borderCollapse: 'collapse', marginTop: 0
344:                       }}>
345:                         <thead style={{ position: 'sticky', top: 0, backgroundColor: '#f3f4f6', zIndex: 1 }}>
346:                           <tr>
347:                             <th style={{ padding: '8px', textAlign: 'center', borderBottom: '1px solid #e5e7eb' }}>K</th>
348:                             <th style={{ padding: '8px', textAlign: 'right', borderBottom: '1px solid #e5e7eb' }}>Coherence Score</th>
349:                             <th style={{ padding: '8px', textAlign: 'right', borderBottom: '1px solid #e5e7eb' }}>Perplexity Score</th>
350:                           </tr>
351:                         </thead>
352:                         <tbody>
353:                           {optimalKResults.map((res, i) => {
354:                             const isBestCoh = suggestedK?.k === res.k;
355:                             const isBestPerp = bestPerplexityK?.k === res.k;
356:                             return (
357:                               <tr key={i} style={{ backgroundColor: (isBestCoh || isBestPerp) ? '#eff6ff' : 'transparent' }}>
358:                                 <td style={{ padding: '8px', borderBottom: '1px solid #f3f4f6', textAlign: 'center', fontWeight: 'bold' }}>{res.k}</td>
359:                                 <td style={{
360:                                   padding: '8px', borderBottom: '1px solid #f3f4f6', textAlign: 'right',
361:                                   color: isBestCoh ? '#2563eb' : '#4b5563', fontWeight: isBestCoh ? 'bold' : 'normal'
362:                                 }}>
363:                                   {res.score.toFixed(4)} {isBestCoh && '⭐'}
364:                                 </td>
365:                                 <td style={{
366:                                   padding: '8px', borderBottom: '1px solid #f3f4f6', textAlign: 'right',
367:                                   color: isBestPerp ? '#059669' : '#4b5563', fontWeight: isBestPerp ? 'bold' : 'normal'
368:                                 }}>
369:                                   {res.perplexity.toFixed(4)} {isBestPerp && '⭐'}
370:                                 </td>
371:                               </tr>
372:                             );
373:                           })}
374:                         </tbody>
375:                       </table>
376:                     </div>
377: 
378:                     <h4 style={{ margin: '0 0 10px 0', fontSize: '14px', color: '#4b5563' }}>Grafik Perbandingan</h4>
379:                     <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '15px', marginBottom: '15px' }}>
380:                       {[
381:                         { dataKey: 'score', name: 'Coherence', color: '#3b82f6', label: '📈 Coherence (Makin Tinggi = Baik)', tColor: '#1d4ed8' },
382:                         { dataKey: 'perplexity', name: 'Perplexity', color: '#10b981', label: '📉 Perplexity (Makin Rendah = Baik)', tColor: '#047857' },
383:                       ].map(({ dataKey, name, color, label, tColor }) => (
384:                         <div key={dataKey} style={{
385:                           width: '100%', height: '220px', backgroundColor: '#fff',
386:                           padding: '15px 10px 10px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #e5e7eb'
387:                         }}>
388:                           <h5 style={{ margin: '0 0 15px 0', fontSize: '12px', color: tColor, textAlign: 'center' }}>{label}</h5>
389:                           <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="80%">
390:                             <LineChart data={optimalKResults} margin={{ top: 0, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}>
391:                               <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" vertical={false} stroke="#e5e7eb" />
392:                               <XAxis dataKey="k" tick={{ fontSize: 11 }} />
393:                               <YAxis tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: color }} axisLine={false} tickLine={false} domain={['auto', 'auto']} />
394:                               <Tooltip contentStyle={{ borderRadius: '8px', fontSize: '12px' }} />
395:                               <Line type="monotone" dataKey={dataKey} name={name} stroke={color} strokeWidth={2} activeDot={{ r: 6 }} />
396:                             </LineChart>
397:                           </ResponsiveContainer>
398:                         </div>
399:                       ))}
400:                     </div>
401: 
402:                     <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '15px' }}>
403:                       <div style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#eff6ff', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '6px', border: '1px solid #bfdbfe' }}>
404:                         <p style={{ margin: '0 0 5px 0', fontSize: '12px', color: '#1d4ed8', fontWeight: '600' }}> Coherence Tertinggi</p>
405:                         <h3 style={{ margin: 0, color: '#1e3a8a', fontSize: '18px' }}>K = {suggestedK?.k}</h3>
406:                         <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '12px', color: '#3b82f6' }}>Score: {suggestedK?.score.toFixed(4)}</p>
407:                       </div>
408:                       <div style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#ecfdf5', padding: '12px', borderRadius: '6px', border: '1px solid #a7f3d0' }}>
409:                         <p style={{ margin: '0 0 5px 0', fontSize: '12px', color: '#047857', fontWeight: '600' }}>Perplexity Terendah</p>
410:                         <h3 style={{ margin: 0, color: '#065f46', fontSize: '18px' }}>K = {bestPerplexityK?.k}</h3>
411:                         <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '12px', color: '#10b981' }}>Score: {bestPerplexityK?.perplexity.toFixed(4)}</p>
412:                       </div>
413:                     </div>
414:                   </div>
415:                 )}
416: 
417:                 {/* Placeholder saat belum ada data */}
418:                 {!optimalKResults && !isSearchingK && !isAnalyzing && (
419:                   <p className="text-muted" style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: '30px 0' }}>
420:                     Klik tombol <strong>Jalankan Analisis</strong> untuk memulai.
421:                   </p>
422:                 )}
423:               </div>
424:             </div>
425:           </div>
426: 
427:           {/* ── Hasil Analisis LDA ── */}
428:           {analysisResult && (
429:             <div style={{ marginTop: '25px', animation: 'fadeIn 0.5s ease-in-out' }}>
430: 
431:               <div className="card-container" style={{
432:                 display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between',
433:                 alignItems: 'center', backgroundColor: '#242424', color: '#fff'
434:               }}>
435:                 <div>
436:                   <h3 style={{ margin: '0 0 5px 0', color: '#fff' }}>Ringkasan Model</h3>
437:                   <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '13px', color: '#9ca3af' }}>
438:                     Judul: {analysisResult.title} &bull; K: {analysisResult.num_topics}
439:                   </p>
440:                 </div>
441:                 <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '30px', textAlign: 'right' }}>
442:                   <div>
443:                     <p style={{ margin: '0 0 5px 0', fontSize: '13px', color: '#9ca3af' }}>Perplexity Score</p>
444:                     <h2 style={{ margin: 0, color: '#10b981' }}>{analysisResult.perplexity_score || '-'}</h2>
445:                   </div>
446:                   <div title="Semakin tinggi nilainya, semakin jelas dan mudah dipahami kata-kata dalam topik tersebut.">
447:                     <p style={{ margin: '0 0 5px 0', fontSize: '13px', color: '#9ca3af', cursor: 'help' }}>Tingkat Kejelasan Topik ℹ️</p>
448:                     <h2 style={{ margin: 0, color: '#3b82f6' }}>{analysisResult.coherence_score}</h2>
449:                   </div>
450:                 </div>
451:               </div>
452: 
453:               {/* KESIMPULAN CERDAS OTOMATIS */}
454:               <div style={{ backgroundColor: '#eff6ff', padding: '15px 20px', borderRadius: '8px', border: '1px solid #bfdbfe', marginBottom: '25px', marginTop: '15px' }}>
455:                 <h4 style={{ margin: '0 0 10px 0', color: '#1d4ed8', fontSize: '15px' }}>💡 Kesimpulan Otomatis</h4>
456:                 <p style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: '14px', lineHeight: '1.6', color: '#1e3a8a' }}>
457:                   Dari ulasan <strong>{analysisResult.title}</strong>, model menemukan <strong>{analysisResult.num_topics} topik utama</strong> yang dibicarakan penonton. 
458:                   Topik yang paling dominan dibicarakan adalah mengenai <strong>{
459:                     Object.entries(analysisResult.overall_distribution).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0]
460:                       ? (interpretations[Object.entries(analysisResult.overall_distribution).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0][0]]?.custom_label || analysisResult.topics?.[Object.entries(analysisResult.overall_distribution).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0][0]]?.auto_label || "Topik 1")
461:                       : "Topik"
462:                   }</strong> (sebanyak {Object.entries(analysisResult.overall_distribution).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0]?.[1] || 0}%).
463:                   Silakan lihat detail kata-kata kuncinya di bawah ini untuk memahami lebih lanjut.
464:                 </p>
465:               </div>
466: 
467:               <h2 className="section-title">Persentase Topik Keseluruhan</h2>
468:               <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '15px', flexWrap: 'wrap', marginBottom: '30px' }}>
469:                 {Object.entries(analysisResult.overall_distribution).map(([topic, pct], idx) => {
470:                   const label = interpretations[topic]?.custom_label || analysisResult.topics?.[topic]?.auto_label;
471:                   return (
472:                     <div key={idx} className="stat-card">
473:                       <h4>{label ? `${topic}: ${label}` : topic}</h4>
474:                       <h2>{pct}%</h2>
475:                     </div>

2: import { AppContext } from '../context/AppContext';
3: import { LineChart, Line, XAxis, YAxis, CartesianGrid, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer } from 'recharts';

5: function Analysis() {
6:   const {

23:   const [editBuffer, setEditBuffer] = useState({ label: '', notes: '' });
24:   const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
25:

27:

29:     return () => { if (pollRef.current) clearInterval(pollRef.current); };

38:           custom_label: topicData.auto_label || '',

54:       const scriptCode = `(function() {
55:         var dist = ${JSON.stringify(distData)};
56:         setInterval(function() {
57:           Object.keys(dist).forEach(function(key) {
58:             var num    = key.replace('Topik ', '');
59:             var circle = document.getElementById('topic' + num);
60:             if (circle) {
61:               var cx = circle.getAttribute('cx');
62:               var cy = circle.getAttribute('cy');
63:               var parent = circle.parentNode;
64:               if (parent) {
65:                 var texts = parent.querySelectorAll('text');
66:                 texts.forEach(function(t) {
67:                   if (t.textContent.trim() === String(num) && t.id.indexOf('custom-pct') === -1) {
68:                     t.style.display = 'none';
69:                   }
70:                 });
71:               }
72:               var customId   = 'custom-pct-' + num;
73:               var customText = document.getElementById(customId);
74:               if (!customText) {
75:                 customText = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "text");
76:                 customText.id = customId;
77:                 customText.setAttribute('text-anchor', 'middle');
78:                 customText.setAttribute('dominant-baseline', 'central');
79:                 customText.setAttribute('fill', '#242424');
80:                 customText.setAttribute('font-size', '16px');
81:                 customText.setAttribute('font-weight', 'bold');
82:                 customText.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
83:                 customText.textContent = dist[key] + '%';
84:                 parent.appendChild(customText);
85:               }
86:               if (cx && cy) {
87:                 customText.setAttribute('x', cx);
88:                 customText.setAttribute('y', cy);
89:               }
90:             }
91:           });
92:         }, 100);
93:       })();`;
94:       newWindow.document.write('<script>' + scriptCode + '<\/script>');

98:     }

116:       });

118:       if (data.status === 'success') {
119:         setInterpretations(prev => ({

131:       setIsSaving(false);

163:         return;
164:       }

238:

250:             const bestPerp = results.reduce((min, c) => c.perplexity < min.perplexity ? c : min, results[0]);

162:         try { const e = await response.json(); errStr = e.error || errStr; } catch (_) { }
163:         setStatus(`Gagal Analisis: ${errStr}`);
164:         showToast(errStr, 'error');
165:         return;
166:       }
167:       const data = await response.json();

169:         setAnalysisResult(data.data);

173:       } else {
174:         setStatus(`Gagal Analisis: ${data.error}`);
175:         showToast(data.error, 'error');
176:       }
177:     } catch {
178:       setStatus("Gagal menghubungi server backend.");
179:       showToast("Tidak dapat terhubung ke server.", 'error');

245:

247:             clearInterval(pollRef.current);
248:             setIsSearchingK(false);
249: 
250:             const results = task.result;
251:             const bestCoh = results.reduce((max, c) => c.score > max.score ? c : max, results[0]);
252:             const bestPerp = results.reduce((min, c) => c.perplexity < min.perplexity ? c : min, results[0]);
253: 
254:             setOptimalKResults(results);
255:             setSuggestedK(bestCoh);
256:             setBestPerplexityK(bestPerp);
257:             setStatus(`Evaluasi selesai. K optimal (Coherence): ${bestCoh.k} (${bestCoh.mode}). Melatih model final...`);
258:             showToast(`Evaluasi K selesai! Melanjutkan analisis dengan K=${bestCoh.k} (${bestCoh.mode})...`, 'info');
259:

144:   const loadSpecificModel = async (targetMode, targetK) => {
145:     if (!movieTitle || !movieTitle.trim()) return;
146:     const cleanTitle = movieTitle.trim().replace(/\s+/g, '_');
147:     const dbKey = `${cleanTitle}_${targetMode}_k${targetK}`;
148:     setIsAnalyzing(true);
149:     try {
150:       const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/saved_movies/${dbKey}`);
151:       if (res.ok) {
152:         const data = await res.json();
153:         if (data.status === 'success' && data.data) {
154:           setAnalysisResult(data.data);
155:           setNumTopics(targetK);
156:           showToast(`Menampilkan hasil N-Gram: ${targetMode.toUpperCase()} (K=${targetK})`, 'info');
157:           setIsAnalyzing(false);
158:           return;
159:         }
160:       }
161:       // Fallback jika belum di-cache: jalankan /analyze
162:       await runAnalyze(targetK, targetMode, optimalKResults);
163:     } catch {
164:       showToast('Gagal memuat model untuk mode N-Gram ini.', 'error');
165:     } finally {

167:     }
168:   };

1: import { useContext, useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
2: import { AppContext } from '../context/AppContext';
3: import { LineChart, Line, XAxis, YAxis, CartesianGrid, Tooltip, ResponsiveContainer } from 'recharts';
4: 
5: function Analysis() {
6:   const {
7:     API_BASE_URL, prepSteps, status, setStatus,
8:     movieTitle, setMovieTitle, numTopics, setNumTopics,
9:     analysisResult, setAnalysisResult,
10:     optimalKResults, setOptimalKResults,
11:     isSearchingK, setIsSearchingK,
12:     uploadedFilename,
13:     showToast,
14:   } = useContext(AppContext);
15: 
16:   const [suggestedK, setSuggestedK] = useState(null);
17:   const [bestPerplexityK, setBestPerplexityK] = useState(null);
18:   const [interpretations, setInterpretations] = useState({});
19:   const [isAnalyzing, setIsAnalyzing] = useState(false);
20:   const [kProgress, setKProgress] = useState({ current: 0, total: 9, currentK: 2 });
21:   // State untuk fitur edit interpretasi per topik
22:   const [editingTopic, setEditingTopic] = useState(null);
23:   const [editBuffer, setEditBuffer] = useState({ label: '', notes: '' });
24:   const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);
25:   // [REVISI] Tab filter mode N-Gram (Unigram, Bigram, Trigram)
26:   const [selectedModeTab, setSelectedModeTab] = useState('all');
27:   // [REVISI UX] Tab tampilan utama vs detail teknis (Awam vs Teknis)
28:   const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('main');
29: 
30:   const pollRef = useRef(null);
31: 
32:   useEffect(() => {
33:     return () => { if (pollRef.current) clearInterval(pollRef.current); };
34:   }, []);
35: 
36:   useEffect(() => {
37:     if (analysisResult?.topics) {
38:       const saved = analysisResult.interpretations || {};
39:       const initial = {};
40:       Object.entries(analysisResult.topics).forEach(([topicName, topicData]) => {
41:         initial[topicName] = saved[topicName] || {
42:           custom_label: topicData.auto_label || '',
43:           notes: topicData.auto_notes || ''
44:         };
45:       });
46:       setInterpretations(initial);
47:     } else {
48:       setInterpretations({});
49:     }
50:   }, [analysisResult]);
51: 
52:   const openFullScreen = () => {
53:     if (!analysisResult?.vis_html) return;
54:     const newWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
55:     if (newWindow) {
56:       newWindow.document.write(analysisResult.vis_html);
57:       const distData = analysisResult.overall_distribution;
58:       const scriptCode = `(function() {
59:         var dist = ${JSON.stringify(distData)};
60:         setInterval(function() {
61:           Object.keys(dist).forEach(function(key) {
62:             var num    = key.replace('Topik ', '');
63:             var circle = document.getElementById('topic' + num);
64:             if (circle) {
65:               var cx = circle.getAttribute('cx');
66:               var cy = circle.getAttribute('cy');
67:               var parent = circle.parentNode;
68:               if (parent) {
69:                 var texts = parent.querySelectorAll('text');
70:                 texts.forEach(function(t) {
71:                   if (t.textContent.trim() === String(num) && t.id.indexOf('custom-pct') === -1) {
72:                     t.style.display = 'none';
73:                   }
74:                 });
75:               }
76:               var customId   = 'custom-pct-' + num;
77:               var customText = document.getElementById(customId);
78:               if (!customText) {
79:                 customText = document.createElementNS("http://www.w3.org/2000/svg", "text");
80:                 customText.id = customId;
81:                 customText.setAttribute('text-anchor', 'middle');
82:                 customText.setAttribute('dominant-baseline', 'central');
83:                 customText.setAttribute('fill', '#242424');
84:                 customText.setAttribute('font-size', '16px');
85:                 customText.setAttribute('font-weight', 'bold');
86:                 customText.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
87:                 customText.textContent = dist[key] + '%';
88:                 parent.appendChild(customText);
89:               }
90:               if (cx && cy) {
91:                 customText.setAttribute('x', cx);
92:                 customText.setAttribute('y', cy);
93:               }
94:             }
95:           });
96:         }, 100);
97:       })();`;
98:       newWindow.document.write('<script>' + scriptCode + '<\/script>');
99:       newWindow.document.close();
100:     } else {
101:       showToast("Pop-up diblokir. Mohon izinkan pop-up pada browser Anda.", 'warning');
102:     }
103:   };
104: 
105:   // [BUG-01] Simpan interpretasi manual ke backend
106:   const saveInterpretation = async (topicName) => {
107:     setIsSaving(true);
108:     try {
109:       const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/update_interpretation`, {
110:         method: 'POST',
111:         headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
112:         body: JSON.stringify({
113:           title:        analysisResult.title,
114:           num_topics:   analysisResult.num_topics,
115:           mode:         analysisResult.ngram_mode || 'bigram',
116:           topic_id:     topicName,
117:           custom_label: editBuffer.label,
118:           notes:        editBuffer.notes,
119:         })
120:       });
121:       const data = await res.json();
122:       if (data.status === 'success') {
123:         setInterpretations(prev => ({
124:           ...prev,
125:           [topicName]: { custom_label: editBuffer.label, notes: editBuffer.notes }
126:         }));
127:         setEditingTopic(null);
128:         showToast(`Label “${topicName}” berhasil disimpan!`, 'success');
129:       } else {
130:         showToast(data.error || 'Gagal menyimpan interpretasi.', 'error');
131:       }
132:     } catch {
133:       showToast('Tidak dapat terhubung ke server.', 'error');
134:     } finally {
135:       setIsSaving(false);
136:     }
137:   };
138: 
139:   // [BUG-08] Potong teks ulasan agar tabel distribusi dokumen tetap ringkas
140:   const truncateText = (text, maxLen = 120) => {
141:     if (!text) return '';
142:     return text.length <= maxLen ? text : text.substring(0, maxLen) + '...';
143:   };
144: 
145:   // Memuat model spesifik berdasarkan mode N-Gram dan K
146:   const loadSpecificModel = async (targetMode, targetK) => {
147:     if (!movieTitle || !movieTitle.trim()) return;
148:     const cleanTitle = movieTitle.trim().replace(/\s+/g, '_');
149:     const dbKey = `${cleanTitle}_${targetMode}_k${targetK}`;
150:     setIsAnalyzing(true);
151:     try {
152:       const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/saved_movies/${dbKey}`);
153:       if (res.ok) {
154:         const data = await res.json();
155:         if (data.status === 'success' && data.data) {
156:           setAnalysisResult(data.data);
157:           setNumTopics(targetK);
158:           showToast(`Menampilkan hasil N-Gram: ${targetMode.toUpperCase()} (K=${targetK})`, 'info');
159:           setIsAnalyzing(false);
160:           return;
161:         }
162:       }
163:       // Fallback jika belum di-cache: jalankan /analyze
164:       await runAnalyze(targetK, targetMode, optimalKResults);
165:     } catch {
166:       showToast('Gagal memuat model untuk mode N-Gram ini.', 'error');
167:     } finally {
168:       setIsAnalyzing(false);
169:     }
170:   };
171: 
172:   // Switch N-Gram Tab secara penuh (100% memisahkan seluruh hasil)
173:   const handleSwitchNgramMode = async (mode) => {
174:     setSelectedModeTab(mode);
175:     if (!optimalKResults) return;
176: 
177:     // Cari K terbaik untuk mode N-Gram ini (berdasarkan Coherence score)
178:     const modeItems = optimalKResults.filter(item => item.mode === mode);
179:     if (modeItems.length > 0) {
180:       const bestItem = modeItems.reduce((max, curr) => curr.score > max.score ? curr : max, modeItems[0]);
181:       await loadSpecificModel(mode, bestItem.k);
182:     }
183:   };
184: 
185:   // Jalankan LDA setelah K diketahui 
186:   const runAnalyze = async (bestK, bestMode, kResults) => {
187:     setIsAnalyzing(true);
188:     setStatus(`Melatih model LDA final dengan K=${bestK} (${bestMode})...`);
189:     setAnalysisResult(null);
190:     try {
191:       const response = await fetch(`${API_BASE_URL}/analyze`, {
192:         method: 'POST',
193:         headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },

828:                               style={{ width: '100%', padding: '6px 10px', borderRadius: '6px', border: '1px solid #d3d3d3', fontSize: '13px', marginBottom: '8px', boxSizing: 'border-box' }}
829:                             />
830:                             <label style={{ fontSize: '12px', fontWeight: '600', color: '#3f3f3f', display: 'block', marginBottom: '4px' }}>Catatan Interpretasi</label>

833:                               onChange={(e) => setEditBuffer(prev => ({ ...prev, notes: e.target.value }))}

836:                             />
837:                             <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '8px' }}>
838:                               <button onClick={() => saveInterpretation(topicName)} disabled={isSaving} className="btn-primary" style={{ fontSize: '12px', padding: '6px 14px', minWidth: 'auto' }}>
839:                                 {isSaving ? 'Menyimpan...' : 'Simpan'}

844:                             </div>
845:                           </>

849:                           </p>
850:                         )}
--- CHUNK 850 to 910 ---
850:                         )}
851:                       </div>
852: 
853:

856:                       <div style={{ marginTop: 'auto' }}>

858:                           <thead>

862:                             </tr>
863:                           </thead>
864:                           <tbody>
865:                             {topicData.words.slice(0, 10).map((w, idx) => {
866:                               const maxWeight = topicData.words[0]?.weight || 1;
867:                               const barPct = Math.min(100, Math.max(10, (w.weight / maxWeight) * 100));
868:                               return (

875:                                       </div>

877:                                         {(w.weight * 100).toFixed(1)}%
878:                                       </span>
879:                                     </div>
880:                                   </td>

883:                             })}
884:                           </tbody>
885:                         </table>
886:                       </div>
887:                     </div>
888:                   );
889:                 })}
890:               </div>

892:               {/* SELURUH ULASAN TERKAIT TOPIK */}

894:                 <h2 className="section-title">Seluruh Ulasan Terkait Tiap Topik</h2>
895:                 <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: '20px' }}>
896:                   {Object.entries(analysisResult.topics || {}).map(([topicName, topicData], idx) => {
897:                     const label = interpretations[topicName]?.custom_label || topicData.auto_label || topicName;
898:                     const relatedDocs = (analysisResult.document_distributions || []).filter(doc => doc.dominant_topic === topicName);
899:                     
900:                     return (

908:
